### [1] [Algebraic Retrieval: Composable Search for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19482)
*Damian Delmas*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出"代数检索"（Algebraic Retrieval），允许AI代理在查询时组合搜索策略，将相关性标准、资格约束和排序偏好统一表达为数学查询，并基于PEM验证了不同实现路径的执行一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索接口固定、缺乏灵活性，代理难以在查询时根据问题动态组合检索策略或在查看结果后修改程序。作者希望让查询成为可组合、可修订的数学表达式，从而支持代理的自主检索规划。

Method: 基于Programmatic Embedding Modulation (PEM)，在检索过程中暴露向量与分数的算术运算，并开放查询接口的操作集合。以对比评分、候选池重排序、加权排序作为可组合查询示例，并给出可执行的SQL与PyTerrier对应实现。评估聚焦于执行一致性（execution parity），而非代理行为或检索质量。

Result: 在公开的Vaswani数据集（11,429篇文档）上，各程序的实现选出了相同的文档集合，分数差异均低于1e-6；仅有一对并列文档在不同评分路径下排序不同。

Conclusion: 代数检索能够支持检索策略的可组合表达，并在不同实现路径间实现高度一致的执行结果。未来可进一步研究代理行为与检索质量层面的影响。

Abstract: Algebraic Retrieval lets AI agents compose search strategies at query time. Relevance criteria, eligibility constraints, and ranking preferences can be expressed together in a mathematical query. The query surface exposes available operations, so an agent can combine them for the question at hand and revise a program after inspecting results. We evaluate execution parity, not agent behavior or retrieval quality. Building on Programmatic Embedding Modulation (PEM), which exposes vector and score arithmetic during retrieval, we demonstrate contrastive scoring, candidate-pool reranking, and weighted ranking as composable queries, alongside executable SQL and PyTerrier counterparts. On the public 11,429-document Vaswani fixture, each program's implementations select the same document set with score differences below 1e-6; one tied pair orders differently across scoring paths.

</details>


### [2] [Beyond Similarity through Zero-Token Geometric Graphs for Multi-Hop RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19622)
*Zeliang Li,Xiaofen Xing,Kailing Guo,Xiangmin Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 G$^3$RAG，一种仅基于文档的图式 RAG 框架，利用几何增益构造图，无需 LLM 调用或生成 token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳 RAG 需要既相关又有足够新颖性的证据来跨越语义鸿沟，但稠密检索偏向语义相似文档，而图式方法依赖昂贵的 LLM 实体抽取且易受噪声连接传播影响。

Method: 离线构图不使用 LLM 调用或生成 token；为每条边赋予几何增益分数 cosθ·sinθ，同时刻画文档表示间的方向一致性与正交性；引入密度感知拓扑惩罚抑制高度连接的枢纽节点；并通过单步受控扩散从过滤后的查询种子向互补证据扩展。

Result: 在 MusiQue、2WikiMultiHopQA 和 HotpotQA 上，使用 Nv-embed-v2 与 Qwen3-8B-embed 两种嵌入设置评估，G$^3$RAG 在平均 F1 和答案文档命中率上均优于所评估的图式基线，平均性能最高提升 4.26 F1 点，MusiQue 上提升 5.76 点，同时消除了实体图方法的构图 token 成本。

Conclusion: 几何结构可在不依赖 LLM 构图的情况下支持高效的多跳证据发现。代码见 https://anonymous.4open.science/r/G3RAG-99D9/。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) requires evidence that remains relevant to a query while introducing enough novelty to bridge semantic gaps. Dense retrieval tends to concentrate on semantically similar documents, whereas graph-based alternatives often depend on costly Large Language Model (LLM) entity extraction and may propagate through noisy connections. We introduce Geometric Gain Graph RAG (G$^3$RAG), a document-only framework whose offline graph construction uses no LLM calls or generated tokens. G$^3$RAG assigns each edge a geometric gain score, $\cosθ\cdot \sinθ$, that jointly captures directional consistency and orthogonality between document representations. A density-aware topological penalty suppresses highly connected hubs, while single-step controlled diffusion expands from filtered query seeds toward complementary evidence. We evaluate G$^3$RAG on MusiQue, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and HotpotQA using Nv-embed-v2 and Qwen3-8B-embed. G$^3$RAG obtains the best average F1 and answer-document hit rate among the evaluated graph-based baselines in both embedding settings, with gains of up to 4.26 F1 points in average performance and 5.76 points on MusiQue. It also removes the graph-construction token cost incurred by entity-based graph methods. These results show that geometric structure can support efficient multi-hop evidence discovery without LLM-based graph construction. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/G3RAG-99D9/

</details>


### [3] [Self-Evolving Search Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19656)
*Sangam Lee,Wonjae Lee,Sunghwan Kim,Deogyong Kim,Jaehoon Kim,Daye Nam,SeongKu Kang,Dongha Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 SELF-INDEX 框架，使检索索引能够无需人工干预地自我演化，通过优化器自主诊断检索不足、选择性修订索引键并验证后再更新，同时利用查询模拟器主动探索额外检索需求，在多种语料与检索器上持续提升检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 智能体处理复杂任务，信息检索愈发重要。检索质量高度依赖索引键能否有效暴露文档知识，但有效的索引表示因检索环境而异，固定优化策略难以稳定奏效。而现有索引演化仍主要依赖人工：需人工诊断失败、改进优化策略并重处理索引。

Method: 提出 SELF-INDEX 框架，让索引无人工干预地自我演化。其 Optimizer 自主诊断检索短板，选择性修订有责任的索引键，并在更新索引前对每次修订进行验证；此外，框架通过 Query Simulator 主动探索额外检索需求，使索引能超越现有可用查询进行演化。

Result: 在多种语料与检索器上，SELF-INDEX 持续提升检索性能，并优于现有索引优化方法。收益还可扩展到下游应用，提升搜索智能体的效果与效率，并帮助智能体记忆系统检索有意义的过往交互。

Conclusion: SELF-INDEX 实现了索引面向检索环境的自主演化，避免人工诊断与优化，并通过主动查询模拟扩展优化空间，在检索与下游智能体任务中均带来一致收益。

Abstract: Information retrieval is increasingly important as LLM agents tackle complex tasks involving diverse information needs. Because retrieval relies on an index that represents each document through index keys, retrieval quality depends heavily on how effectively these keys expose the knowledge contained in each document. However, effective index representations vary across retrieval environments, making it difficult for any fixed optimization strategy to perform consistently. Yet evolving an index to its retrieval environment remains largely human-driven, requiring humans to diagnose retrieval failures, refine the optimization strategy, and reprocess the index accordingly. We propose SELF-INDEX, a framework that enables an index to self-evolve without human intervention. Its Optimizer autonomously diagnoses retrieval shortfalls, selectively revises the responsible index keys, and validates each revision before updating the index. Beyond reacting to observed retrieval demands, SELF-INDEX proactively explores additional demands through a Query Simulator, allowing the index to evolve beyond the queries already available for optimization. Across diverse corpora and retrievers, SELF-INDEX consistently improves retrieval performance while outperforming existing index optimization methods. We further show that these benefits extend to downstream applications, improving the effectiveness and efficiency of search agents and helping agent memory systems retrieve useful past interactions.

</details>


### [4] [Dense Feature Representation over Sequence Modeling: A Solution to the KDD Cup 2026 UniRec Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19787)
*Yi Zhang,Weiliang Ji*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍KDD Cup 2026腾讯UniRec挑战赛（3482万条记录工业CVR预测）第10名方案，通过15步单变量优化将测试AUC从0.813237提升到0.827816，最终提交达0.828535，并指出稠密特征表示与优化器才是AUC提升的核心驱动力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在工业级CVR预测中，究竟哪些机制真正影响留出集AUC，以及序列建模、稠密表示、优化策略各自的真实贡献。

Method: 以官方PCVRHyFormer基线为起点，构建15步单变量链式优化；对完整模型进行留一消融（leave-one-out）实验，并设计抗记忆与高基数ID改动、时间有序重划分等对照实验评估泛化风险。

Result: 测试AUC从0.813237升至0.827816，最终提交0.828535；消融显示移除稠密特征表示栈损失0.0095 AUC、移除正交化优化器损失0.0028，而各序列建模组件（合并单流骨干、极性通道、辅助头、逐token FFN）损失均不超过0.0005（处于±0.0004种子波动带内）。同时发现行组训练/验证划分共享同一时间窗，使验证AUC高估排行榜约0.014。

Conclusion: 在CVR大规模场景下，稠密表示与优化策略而非更精细的序列建模决定AUC表现；评估结论必须依赖留出排行榜，行组划分的验证AUC存在系统性高估，且该偏差源于dump间分布漂移并在时间有序重划分后依然存在。

Abstract: We describe our 10th-place solution to the KDD Cup 2026 Tencent UniRec Challenge, industrial click-to-conversion (CVR) prediction over 34.82M records, and we ask which mechanisms actually move held-out AUC. Starting from the official PCVRHyFormer baseline, a 15-step single-variable chain raises test AUC from 0.813237 to 0.827816, and our final submission reaches 0.828535. A leave-one-out ablation from the full model attributes the gain: removing the dense-feature representation stack costs 0.0095 AUC and removing the orthogonalized optimizer costs 0.0028, while no sequence-modeling component (merged single-stream backbone, polarity channel, auxiliary head, per-token FFN) costs more than 0.0005, within or adjacent to a $\pm$0.0004 seed band. We also report a generalization hazard: the row-group train/validation split shares one time window, so validation AUC overstates the leaderboard by about 0.014; anti-memorization and high-cardinality-ID changes even invert sign against it, a divergence that traces to dump-to-dump distribution shift and survives a time-ordered re-split. Dense representation and optimization, not finer sequence modeling, drive CVR AUC at this scale, and verdicts must come from the held-out leaderboard.

</details>


### [5] [Reproducing Transparent and Scrutable Recommendations: Exploring Open-Weight Models via Natural-Language User Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19831)
*Noah Mamié,Laurin van den Bergh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 复现研究验证了基于生成式自然语言用户画像的推荐系统（UPR）的核心发现，同时通过消融、多随机种子与机制可解释性分析发现画像扰动对预测的影响是全局评分偏移而非按类型的选择性改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原论文提出用生成的用户画像提升推荐系统的透明性与可审查性，但其可靠性与画像干预的机制尚不明确，需要独立复现与更严格的稳定性、可解释性检验。

Method: 复现原研究流程（从电影与住宿领域的原始评论文本合成自然语言用户画像）；扩展实验包括系统性上下文消融、五个不同随机种子的多种子稳定性评估，以及使用 nnsight 框架进行的机制可解释性分析（在反事实画像扰动下探测内部表征）。

Result: 成功复现原研究核心结论：UPR 在其测试集重排协议下具有竞争性表现，并提升推荐透明度。画像扰动确实改变预测，但预测评分在不同类型间均匀偏移，未检出按类型的选择性效应，排序结果即使直接进行激活引导也不改变；该现象可归因于评分回归目标而非画像接口本身，排序目标模型在该任务上明显更优。

Conclusion: UPR 的透明度优势得到验证，但自然语言画像的可干预性受限于底层评分回归目标；未来应以排序目标建模来真正实现可控、可解释的画像驱动推荐。

Abstract: In this reproducibility study, we investigate the transparency and scrutability of recommender systems enhanced by incorporating generated natural-language user profiles that represent user preferences. The original paper explores the synthesis of user profiles from raw user-generated review text across domains such as movies and accommodations (Amazon Movies & TV, TripAdvisor). Crucially, these natural-language user profiles enable direct user interaction and intervention, allowing users to customize recommendations by correcting misattributed preferences or addressing cold-start settings. We successfully reproduce the core findings of the original study. Additionally, we extend the evaluation by conducting systematic context ablation experiments, multi-seed stability across five distinct random seeds to establish statistical reliability, and a mechanistic interpretability analysis using the nnsight framework to probe internal model representations under counterfactual profile perturbations. Our findings verify the original paper's claim that User Profile Recommendation (UPR) achieves competitive performance under its test-set reranking protocol and makes recommendations more transparent. Perturbing the natural-language profiles does change predictions, but it shifts predicted ratings uniformly across genres with no detectable genre-selective effect, leaving rankings unchanged even under direct activation steering. We trace this back to the rating-regression objective rather than the profile interface, with ranking-objective models clearly exceeding in this task.

</details>


### [6] [The Missing Complement: State-Conditioned Minimal Sufficient Evidence for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20050)
*Zhexi Feng,Ruiyi Zhang,Yongbo Yang,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文将编码智能体的证据检索重新表述为"状态条件下的最小充分证据恢复"，提出MSS-Complement方法，将证据获取视为集合构建而非排序，在SERBench上显著优于嵌入重排序基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在处理问题中途已读过检索器排名最高的许多内容；相关性按段落评分，但"充分性"属于整个集合。排序器可能用同一必要事实的多个变体填满预算，却仍无法支撑决策。因此需要从智能体当前状态出发，恢复其下一步决策仍缺失的支持证据。

Method: 提出状态条件下的最小充分证据恢复任务，并构建SERBench（来自45个仓库的500个留存状态，记录智能体已见内容，仅对覆盖当前决策所需全部事实的证据集合计分）。方法MSS-Complement将获取视为集合构建：通过三次语义调用分别提出联合充分集、搜索其缺失部分，并在6,144 token内返回4-8个完整源单元。

Result: 在标定数据上固定的单一配置，五项时对73.0%的状态恢复完整集合，八项时达80.6%；对比Qwen3嵌入重排序为61.4%和72.4%。仅按相似度排序的匹配对照达66.6%，说明增益来自集合级策略而非计算量。在冻结仓库源、无金标池条件下领先5.0点。在AMA-Bench上以缩小76.2%的答案提示达到比该基准记忆智能体高2.08点的准确率。从完整集合中移除一个必要组，在两个执行器下分别损失12.3和11.1点的修复定位精度。

Conclusion: 面向智能体的检索应被表述为"恢复决策所缺失的内容"，而非"对问题相似度重新排序"。

Abstract: A coding agent halfway through an issue has already read much of what a retriever ranks highest. Relevance is scored per passage, but sufficiency belongs to the set: a ranker can fill its budget with variants of one required fact and leave the decision unsupported. We formulate state-conditioned minimal sufficient evidence recovery: given a captured agent state, recover a compact evidence combination that supplies the support its next decision still lacks. SERBench measures this on 500 held-out states from 45 repositories, recording what the agent has seen and crediting only sets that cover every fact the current decision was annotated to require. MSS-Complement treats acquisition as set construction, not ranking. Three semantic calls propose a jointly sufficient set, search for what it lacks, and return 4-8 intact source units within 6,144 tokens. One configuration, fixed on calibration data, recovers a complete set for 73.0% of those states at five items and 80.6% at eight, against 61.4% and 72.4% for Qwen3 embedding with reranking. A matched control ranking by similarity alone reaches 66.6%, placing the gain in the set-level policy, not the computation. From frozen repository source with no gold-derived pool, the lead is 5.0 points. On AMA-Bench it answers from a 76.2% smaller answer prompt, with accuracy 2.08 points above that benchmark's own memory agent. Removing one required group from an otherwise complete set costs 12.3 and 11.1 points of repair-localization precision under two executors. Retrieval for agents is better posed as recovering what a decision lacks than re-ranking what an issue resembles.

</details>


### [7] [Think Thrice Before Reranking: Multi-perspective Evidence and Reasoning Integration for Text Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20131)
*Lijun Liu,Zhengzong Chen,Wenyan Li,Yuanyuan Zhao,Fei Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 MERIT-Rank 框架，通过多视角推理轨迹建模和渐进式训练策略提升文本重排序的鲁棒性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型推理的重排序方法主要依赖单一推理轨迹，导致排序结果易受推理错误影响，且难以建模文档相关性背后的多元信号。为突破这一局限，作者希望引入互补的多重推理路径来增强重排序的稳健性。

Method: 提出 MERIT-Rank 框架，构建多轨迹推理空间（MTRS），从多个视角评估查询-文档相关性；设计联合重排序器，将多条推理路径整合为统一排序决策；并开发渐进式排序策略优化（PRPO）训练框架，通过分阶段优化目标稳定推理轨迹并持续提升排序质量。

Result: 在推理密集型与传统检索基准上，MERIT-Rank 均持续优于竞争性基线。其 4B 模型在 BRIGHT 基准上显著超越大多数 7B 甚至 32B 的重排序器。

Conclusion: 多视角推理轨迹与渐进式训练相结合能有效提升文本重排序的鲁棒性和效果，且较小的模型规模即可达到甚至超越更大模型的性能。

Abstract: Reasoning-based reranking with Large Language Models (LLMs) has shown promising improvements in text ranking. However, current methods predominantly rely on a single reasoning trajectory, resulting in rankings that are susceptible to reasoning errors and inherently constrained in modeling the multifaceted signals underlying document relevance. To resolve this dilemma, we propose MERIT-Rank(Multi-perspective Evidence and Reasoning Integration for Text Reranking), a framework that models complementary reasoning trajectories to improve reranking robustness. MERIT-Rank formulates a Multi-Trajectory Reasoning Space (MTRS) that evaluates query-document relevance from multiple perspectives and introduces a joint reranker that consolidates these reasoning paths into a unified ranking decision. We further develop Progressive Rank Policy Optimization (PRPO), a progressive training framework that stabilizes reasoning trajectories while continually improving ranking quality through staged optimization objectives. Experiments on both reasoning-intensive and traditional retrieval benchmarks show that MERIT-Rank consistently achieves superior performance over competitive baselines. The 4B model notably outperforms most 7B and even 32B rerankers on BRIGHT.

</details>


### [8] [FacetCRS: Multi-Faceted Preference Learning for Pricking Filter Bubbles in Conversational Recommender System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20175)
*Yongsen Zheng,Ziliang Chen,Jinghui Qin,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出FacetCRS，一种多面向偏好学习的会话推荐框架，通过自然语言对话中的及时用户-物品交互来打破过滤气泡，将用户偏好建模为实体、词、上下文和评论四个面向，实验证明其在缓解过滤气泡和提升推荐质量上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过滤气泡是推荐系统中的突出问题，用户被暴露在狭窄且强化的信息范围内，缺乏多样性内容。现有研究多在静态或相对静态的推荐场景中考察过滤气泡，但在真实在线推荐中，用户与系统之间的反馈循环会随时间不断加强过滤气泡。

Method: 提出FacetCRS新范式，在会话推荐系统（CRS）中通过自然语言对话的及时用户-物品交互来打破过滤气泡。考虑多样化的用户偏好与意图，自动将用户偏好建模为多面向，包括实体面向、词面向、上下文面向和评论面向，以捕捉多样且动态的用户偏好。该框架为端到端CRS框架，可自适应学习不同层级偏好面向的表征及多种类型的外部知识。

Result: 在两个公开基准数据集上的大量实验表明，所提方法在缓解过滤气泡和提升CRS推荐质量方面均达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: FacetCRS通过多面向偏好学习和会话交互有效打破了过滤气泡，验证了动态、多维度建模用户偏好在缓解过滤气泡方面的有效性。

Abstract: The filter bubble is a notorious issue in Recommender Systems (RSs), which describes the phenomenon whereby users are exposed to a limited and narrow range of information or content that reinforces their existing dominant preferences and beliefs. This results in a lack of exposure to diverse and varied content. Many existing works have predominantly examined filter bubbles in static or relatively-static recommendation settings. However, filter bubbles will be continuously intensified over time due to the feedback loop between the user and the system in the real-world online recommendation. To address these issues, we propose a novel paradigm, Multi-Facet Preference Learning for Pricking Filter Bubbles in Conversational Recommender System (FacetCRS), which aims to burst filter bubbles in the conversational recommender system (CRS) through timely user-item interactions via natural language conversations. By considering diverse user preferences and intentions, FacetCRS automatically model user preference into multi-facets, including entity-, word-, context-, and review-facet, to capture diverse and dynamic user preferences to prick filter bubbles in the CRS. It is an end-to-end CRS framework to adaptively learn representations of various levels of preference facet and diverse types of external knowledge. Extensive experiments on two publicly available benchmark datasets demonstrate that our proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance in mitigating filter bubbles and enhancing recommendation quality in CRS.

</details>


### [9] [Reasoning Quality Matters: Combating Reasoning Collapse in LLM-based Embedding Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20563)
*Zihan Gong,Xiaohan Ye,Jiangchao Yao,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CoFree两阶段框架，将LLM推理逐步融入查询与文档嵌入优化，缓解"推理崩溃"，CoFree-4B在22个数据集上平均提升2.8 nDCG@10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM嵌入方法要么把嵌入学习当作被动特征提取，要么通过指令跟随利用推理来优化嵌入。但专门面向嵌入目标会压制有用的推理生成，或产生与检索无关的文本，作者将这两类退化统称为"推理崩溃"（reasoning collapse），需要一种既保留推理质量又优化嵌入的方法。

Method: CoFree两阶段框架。第一阶段：参考引导的监督微调，恢复基础嵌入模型的推理能力并保留其表示强度。第二阶段：引入双奖励（嵌入导向奖励与推理导向奖励），在强化学习中保证对相关性进行细粒度推理并朝向嵌入目标，实现"端点耦合"优化，将嵌入学习从静态对齐转变为高质量的推理引导搜索过程。

Result: 在MTEB与BRIGHT的22个数据集上，CoFree-4B相较Qwen3-Embedding-4B平均绝对提升2.8个nDCG@10点；真实检索系统的在线实验也显示一致的收益。代码、RTED与模型检查点将公开。

Conclusion: CoFree通过两阶段渐进式整合LLM推理，有效缓解推理崩溃，显著提升检索嵌入性能，并在离线与在线场景中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently shown strong potential for producing context-rich text embeddings for retrieval. Most existing methods either treat embedding learning as passive feature extraction or exploit LLM reasoning through instruction following for better embedding optimization. However, specialization toward embedding objectives can suppress useful reasoning generation or produce retrieval-irrelevant text. We refer to these two forms of degradation as reasoning collapse. To address this issue, we propose CoFree (Collapse-Free Reasoning Embedding), a two-stage framework that progressively integrates LLM reasoning into query and document embedding optimization while preserving reasoning quality. At the first stage, CoFree applies reference-guided supervised fine-tuning to restore the reasoning ability and retain representational strength of the foundation embedding model. At the second stage, we introduce dual rewards, an embedding-oriented reward and a reasoning-oriented reward, to guarantee fine-grained reasoning of the relevance toward the embedding goal in reinforcement learning. This endpoint-coupled optimization transforms embedding learning from static alignment into a high-quality reasoning-guided search process for retrieval. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of CoFree, with CoFree-4B achieving an average absolute improvement of 2.8 nDCG@10 points over Qwen3-Embedding-4B across 22 datasets from MTEB and BRIGHT. Online experiments in a real-world retrieval system further show consistent gains. Code, RTED, and model checkpoints will be made publicly available.

</details>

### [10] [Detecting Soft Errors in Parallel Software with LLM-tuned Instruction Duplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19531)
*Yafan Huang,Guanpeng Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出 PaRID 软错误检测框架，仅需编译期工作即可检测多线程并行程序的软错误，显著降低保护开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行程序软错误检测面临两大挑战：需支持串行与并行混合区域，且需在不依赖高成本动态剖析的前提下降低性能开销。

Method: 结合并行感知代码变换与 LLM 调优的性能建模，并依据离线表征研究得出的八条可泛化发现，采用并行指令复制（PaRID）的软件导向框架，编译期完成全部工作。

Result: 在 NPB 基准测试上，PaRID 将保护开销平均从 162.79% 降至 59.84%，最高实现 5 倍加速，同时保持完整的错误检测有效性。

Conclusion: PaRID 通过编译期优化与 LLM 性能建模，在保证检测效果的同时大幅降低并行程序软错误检测开销。

Abstract: We propose PaRID (PaRallel Instruction Duplication), a software-directed soft error detection framework that requires only compile-time effort for multithreading parallel programs. PaRID addresses two key challenges: supporting parallel programs with mixed serial and parallel regions and minimizing performance overhead without relying on costly dynamic profiling. It combines parallel-aware code transformation with LLM-tuned performance modeling, guided by eight generalizable findings from an offline characterization study, to enable fast soft error detection in parallel applications. Evaluation on NPB benchmarks shows that PaRID reduces protection overhead from 162.79% to 59.84% on average and achieves up to 5x speedup while maintaining full error detection effectiveness.

</details>


### [11] [Syndrome Decoding for Silent Data Corruption in Quantized Integer GPU Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19743)
*Pranav Napolean,Vikas Srivastava,Napolean Periathambi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SProbe是一种针对量化神经网络GPU推理中INT32累加器静默数据损坏的验证与修复内核，结合Freivalds门控与Reed-Solomon级联解码，实现高概率检测与精确修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化神经网络推理在GPU张量核心上运行整数矩阵乘法，其INT32累加器既无奇偶校验也无ECC，瞬态故障会产生有效但错误的整数且不触发中断。基于校验和的ABFT虽能检测此类静默数据损坏，但其判定是二元的，无法定位损坏元素或幅值，且无权重行列校验和对两轴抵消的错误天然失明。

Method: 提出SProbe尾随验证内核，读取未修改的厂商GEMM输出。采用61位素数域上三个独立求值点的随机化Freivalds门控，漏检概率不超过2^{-141}。当门控触发时，使用三个素数上的逐行幂和校验子，通过Berlekamp-Massey、Chien搜索和Forney的Reed-Solomon链解码，恢复每行最多四个碰撞错误的列位置与精确幅值，随后原地修复累加器或重算GEMM。

Result: 在NVIDIA H100上，SProbe在七类故障和四种矩阵规模下检测出所有注入故障，包括TR-ABFT从不能检测的构造模式以及加权网格码能检测但无法纠正的模式。门控在N=16384时占cuBLASLt GEMM时间的49%，在N=65536时占11%。在INT8医疗LLM中，保护消除了所有观察到的静默损坏，吞吐成本为30%。测量还显示重算在每个测试配置中均快于原地恢复，诊断而非修复主导恢复成本，并报告了验证验证器本身时发现的缺陷。

Conclusion: SProbe能有效检测并修复量化神经网络推理中的静默数据损坏，检测概率高、覆盖故障类型广，虽有一定开销但可在医疗LLM等场景中消除静默损坏，且重算优于原地恢复。

Abstract: Quantized neural network inference runs integer matrix multiplications on GPU tensor cores, and the INT32 accumulators inside those cores have neither parity nor ECC. A transient fault in this datapath returns a valid but wrong integer and raises no interrupt. Checksum based Algorithm Based Fault Tolerance (ABFT) can detect such silent data corruptions (SDCs), but its verdict is binary. It cannot identify the corrupted element or its magnitude, and unweighted row and column checksums are blind by construction to errors that cancel on both axes. We present SProbe, a trailing verification kernel that reads the output of an unmodified vendor GEMM. A randomized Freivalds gate with three independent evaluation points in a 61 bit prime field misses a nonzero error with probability at most $2^{-141}$. When the gate fires, per row power sum syndromes over three primes are decoded with the Reed Solomon chain of Berlekamp Massey, Chien search, and Forney, recovering the column and exact magnitude of up to four colliding errors per row. SProbe then repairs the accumulator in place or recomputes the GEMM. On an NVIDIA H100, SProbe detects every injected fault across seven fault classes and four matrix sizes, including constructed patterns that TR-ABFT never detects and patterns that a weighted grid code detects but cannot correct. The gate costs 49% of the cuBLASLt GEMM time at N=16384 and 11% at N=65536. In an INT8 medical LLM, protection eliminates all observed silent corruptions at a 30% throughput cost. Our measurements also show that recomputation is faster than in place recovery in every configuration we tested, that diagnosis rather than repair dominates recovery cost, and we report the defects we found while validating the verifier itself.

</details>


### [12] [Sketching the Error, Not the Product: Post Hoc Fault Recovery for Half Precision GPU Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19758)
*Pranav Napolean,Vikas Srivastava,Napolean Periathambi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FP-Sketch 是一种用于张量核心 GEMM（半精度输入、FP32 累加与输出）的故障验证器，可在不改动内核的前提下检测并定位静默数据损坏（SDC）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速器缺陷引发的静默数据损坏已干扰大规模训练，但现有缓解手段通常以整节点为粒度；针对单个 GEMM 的 ABFT 需融合进内核或编码操作数，且每个校验位最多只能定位一个错误。

Method: 在未修改的张量核心 GEMM 之后运行验证器：用求和 sketch 检测每次调用的损坏；用哈希一阶矩 sketch 并结合独立重计算来定位多个损坏条目，且构造上无假阳性，每个故障给出坐标与幅度。通过 NVBit 进行指令级注入，并测量浮点 sketch 噪声。

Result: 浮点下限制定位的是 sketch 噪声而非桶碰撞，噪声常数取决于 BLAS 与操作数格式，方阵乘积桶数需按 n^2.57 增长。按实测噪声而非拟合幂次设定桶数，可将八个 transformer 形状的恢复率从 0.402 提升到 1.000，运行时测量噪声可自适应内核与模型。NVBit 显示活跃累加器中的翻转常仅为典型条目的 2%–9%，是输出侧注入无法产生的分布；输出侧注入可恢复全部故障，而按典型幅度设定引擎在 NVBit 下仅恢复 0.550，按实测幅度设定则恢复 1.000。在 Llama-2-7B 上，守护 MLP 下投影可消除 2048 次位翻转造成的困惑度损伤的 99.4%（BF16）与 99.9%（FP16），干净路径探针耗时 0.78–3.06 ms，而 GEMM 为 0.35–12.47 ms。

Conclusion: FP-Sketch 提供了一种非侵入、可检测并可定位多错误且无假阳性的 GEMM 故障验证方案，其桶数应依据实测噪声而非 n 的拟合幂次来设定；对故障幅度的实测自适应能显著提升恢复率，并在 Llama-2-7B 上以较低开销大幅降低位翻转对困惑度的损伤。

Abstract: Silent data corruption (SDC) from defective accelerators now interrupts large scale training, yet deployed mitigations act on whole nodes. Algorithm based fault tolerance (ABFT) for a single GEMM has to be fused into the kernel or encode the operands, and it localizes at most one error per checksum. We present FP-Sketch, a verifier that runs after an unmodified tensor core GEMM whose half precision operands are accumulated and delivered at FP32. A sum sketch detects corruption on every call. Hashed first moment sketches, confirmed by independent recomputation, then localize several corrupted entries with no false positives by construction, and each fault yields a coordinate and a magnitude for fleet diagnosis. In floating point, sketch noise rather than bucket collisions limits localization. We measure that noise and find that its constant depends on the BLAS and the operand format and that the bucket count must grow as $n^{2.57}$ for a square product. Sizing the bucket count by measured noise rather than by a fitted power of $n$ raises recovery on eight transformer shapes from 0.402 to 1.000, and measuring the noise at run time adapts the bucket count to the kernel and the model. Instruction level injection with NVBit shows that upsets in a live accumulator are often only 2 to 9% of a typical entry, a population that output side injection cannot produce. Output side injection recovers every fault, while under NVBit the same engine sized for faults of typical magnitude recovers 0.550, and sizing for the measured magnitudes restores 1.000. On Llama-2-7B, guarding the MLP down projections removes 99.4% (BF16) and 99.9% (FP16) of the perplexity damage caused by 2048 bit flips, and the clean path probe costs 0.78 to 3.06 ms against GEMMs of 0.35 to 12.47 ms.

</details>


### [13] [Safe Exploration of Arbitrary Dynamic Dangerous Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19845)
*Caterina Feletti,Paola Flocchini,Giuseppe Prencipe,Nicola Santoro*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究存在黑洞的危险动态网络中的安全探索问题，证明了一定数量无记忆智能体可在半同步调度下无先验知识地永久安全探索，并给出带ID与持久记忆智能体的终止算法，同时证明无法准确标记通往黑洞的端口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅研究任意动态网络中的探索问题，且依赖时间连通性假设，但未考虑网络中可能存在黑洞的危险情形。本文旨在将探索研究扩展到含黑洞的动态网络，保证智能体不被永久困住地探索所有安全节点。

Method: 在时间连通性假设下建模含一个或多个黑洞的动态网络；先分析无记忆（oblivious）智能体在无网络与团队先验知识、无ID条件下的半同步调度探索；再设计带唯一ID、持久记忆且已知安全节点数的智能体安全探索并终止的算法；并给出关于端口标记不可能性的证明。

Result: 在所需（且充分）数量智能体条件下，无记忆智能体可无先验知识、无ID地永久探索所有安全节点；带ID与持久记忆的智能体可安全探索并终止；但两类情形下均无法正确标记仅通往黑洞的端口。

Conclusion: 安全探索在动态网络中可行且所需智能体数量与时间连通性相关，但准确识别并标记黑洞端口在动态网络模型下不可实现。

Abstract: Given a team of agents on the nodes of a graph-based network, the exploration problem requires each node to be visited by at least one agent. In the classical distributed setting of static networks, agents do not know the topology of the network; in the more recently investigated setting of dynamic networks, they may have prior knowledge about the graph class (e.g., trees, tori, rings) or other parameters (e.g., number of nodes). Gotoh et al. (2021) are the first to study the exploration problem of arbitrary dynamic networks, under the necessary minimal assumption that any two nodes will be connected by a temporal path infinitely often (temporal connectivity assumption). In this paper, we extend their study of exploration under temporal connectivity by considering dangerous dynamic networks, i.e., containing possibly one or more black holes. Whenever an agent enters a black hole, it will be trapped forever. The safe exploration problem requires a team to explore all the safe nodes, ensuring that at least one agent will never be trapped in a black hole. We first prove that, given the necessary (and sufficient) number of agents, a team of oblivious agents can perpetually explore the safe nodes without any prior knowledge of the network or the team, without agent or node IDs, under semi-synchronous schedulers. Then, we provide an algorithm that enables agents to safely explore the network and terminate. In this case, agents are equipped with unique IDs and persistent memory, and they know the number of safe nodes. Yet, in both cases, we prove that it is impossible for a team of agents on dynamic networks to correctly mark only the ports leading to black holes.

</details>


### [14] [Xronos: Heterogeneity-Aware Tensor Parallelism for Collaborative LLM Fine-Tuning on Edge CPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19909)
*Wonmi Choi,Sunjae Park,Dohyeok Kwon,Zhixiong Niu,Yeonho Yoo,Chuck Yoo,Gyeongsik Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Xronos 是面向异构 CPU 边缘设备的协同微调框架，采用张量并行（TP）并结合轻量级性能分析与异构感知张量划分，相比 SOTA 降低微调时间 18%–56%，设备空闲时间降低约 5.9 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SOTA 协同微调技术主要面向 GPU 边缘设备并依赖流水线并行（PP）。但许多边缘平台（IoT 网关、智能家居中枢、车载计算机）以 CPU 为主。论文发现 PP 在 CPU 边缘设备上无效，因为同一 CPU 同时承担计算与通信，导致严重 CPU 争用，计算停滞比例平均高出 GPU 设备 5.75 倍。而 TP 虽可缓解争用，却假设设备同构，在异构 CPU 设备上会导致快节点等待慢节点，空闲率高达 34%。

Method: Xronos 以张量并行（TP）作为执行骨干，结合轻量级性能分析（profiling）与异构感知的张量划分（heterogeneity-aware tensor partitioning），以缓解拖后腿（straggler）瓶颈。

Result: 在多种设备、模型和基准任务上，Xronos 相比 SOTA 技术将微调时间降低 18%（对比 TP）至 56%（对比 PP），设备空闲时间比例降低约 5.9 倍，同时保持精度。

Conclusion: 针对 CPU 边缘设备的异构协同微调需求，Xronos 用 TP 替代 PP 并引入异构感知划分，有效缓解 CPU 争用与慢节点等待问题，在保持精度的同时显著提升微调效率。

Abstract: Collaborative fine-tuning on edge devices adapts large language models to domain-specific data while keeping each device's data local. State-of-the-art (SOTA) collaborative fine-tuning techniques are largely designed for GPU-based edge devices and rely on pipeline parallelism (PP). However, many edge platforms, including IoT gateways, smart-home hubs, and in-vehicle computers, are primarily CPU-based. This paper reports that PP is ineffective on CPU-based edge devices because the same CPU handles both model computation and communication, which causes severe CPU contention. Our analysis shows that this leads to 5.75$\times$ higher computation stall ratios than on GPU devices on average. Tensor parallelism (TP) can alleviate this contention by separating computation and communication, but existing TP techniques assume homogeneous devices. On heterogeneous CPU edge devices, we find that this assumption causes faster workers to remain idle for up to 34% while waiting for slower devices at synchronization points. To address the limitations, we propose Xronos, a collaborative fine-tuning framework for heterogeneous CPU edge devices. Xronos uses TP as its execution backbone and combines lightweight profiling with heterogeneity-aware tensor partitioning to reduce the straggler bottleneck. Across diverse devices, models, and benchmark tasks, Xronos reduces fine-tuning time by 18% (TP) to 56% (PP) and the ratio of device idle time by $\sim$5.9$\times$ over SOTA techniques, while maintaining the accuracy.

</details>


### [15] [The Life of a Token: from Words to Bits on the Wire](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19924)
*Davide Avesani,Pengwenlong Gu,Sotiris Skaperas,Stefano Secci*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该教程梳理了从文本到网络流量的完整链路，揭示LLM训练中语言如何转化为HPC系统内的通信流，并分析其网络通信需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM参数规模达数十亿至数万亿，训练需跨数千个互连加速器展开，底层通信网络成为关键却常被忽视的组件。作者希望揭开这一"黑箱"，帮助读者理解网络需求如何由模型与文本驱动。

Method: 结合架构分析、解析式流量模型与数值示例，以但丁《神曲》为具体文本案例，追踪词→token→向量→bit流的转化过程，说明模型架构、分词、嵌入与并行策略如何决定网络交换数据的量、结构与时序。

Result: 刻画了LLM训练在各转化环节产生的通信流量特征，展示了并行策略等设计对网络负载形态的定量影响，并给出支持"从文本到模型"训练所需的网络要求洞见。

Conclusion: 通过"从词到网络流量"的旅程讲解，去神秘化地呈现LLM训练中的通信机制，为理解并设计支撑大规模训练的网络基础设施提供实用指导。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) transform vast collections of unstructured text into semantic patterns used for language generation and reasoning tasks. Behind their ease of use lies a complex process: words become tokens, tokens become vectors, and vectors ultimately give rise to streams of bits that flow through High-Performance Computing (HPC) systems. As modern LLMs grow to billions or trillions of parameters, this path increasingly unfolds across thousands of interconnected accelerators, making the underlying communication fabric a critical and often opaque component of model training. This tutorial aims to walk the reader through the journey from words to network traffic, shedding light on how language is translated into communication flows within HPC training systems. Using concrete examples from Dante's Divine Comedy, we illustrate how model architecture, tokenization, embeddings, and parallelization strategies shape the volume, structure, and timing of data exchanged across the network. We combine architectural analysis with analytical traffic models and numerical examples to characterize the communication requirements of LLM training. We try to demystify how words travel across the network and provide practical insights into the network requirements needed to support the journey from text to trained model.

</details>


### [16] [VERA: Reinforcement Learning for Dynamic Memory Scaling of HPC Workloads in Kubernetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19936)
*Ade Pramono,Jie Ren,Ivy Peng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于强化学习的纵向内存扩缩容推荐器 VERA，将内存缩放建模为马尔可夫决策过程，在 3353 条真实 Prometheus 轨迹上训练，并在 GKE 集群上验证其优于默认 VPA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPC 工作负载运行在 Kubernetes 上时，内存过度配置导致资源利用率低下；默认 VPA 无法预测首次运行的 HPC 作业中由阶段驱动的内存峰值，因此需要一种能够动态、主动预测内存需求的方法。

Method: 将纵向内存扩缩容形式化为马尔可夫决策过程，基于 3353 条真实 Prometheus 监控轨迹训练强化学习智能体，形成推荐器 VERA。

Result: 在真实 Google Kubernetes Engine 集群上，使用 LAMMPS、图分析、内存分析和 MLPerf 3D-UNet 进行评测：RL 智能体回收了 31.6% 的可用内存余量，且最多仅发生一次 OOM；相同运行下 VPA 表现为 -7.9%（反而增加内存配置），并有 30 次运行的推荐不足以避免 OOM。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于观测驱动的强化学习推荐器在动态内存扩缩容方面可优于回顾式启发式方法。

Abstract: Memory over-provisioning results in resource underutilization when HPC workloads run on Kubernetes. The default Vertical Pod Autoscaler (VPA) cannot anticipate phase-driven memory spikes for first-run HPC jobs. In this work, we present a reinforcement learning (RL) recommender VERA that formulates vertical memory scaling as a Markov Decision Process and trains an agent on 3353 real Prometheus traces. Evaluated on a live Google Kubernetes Engine cluster using LAMMPS, graph analytics, in-memory analytics, and MLPerf 3D-UNet, the RL agent reclaims 31.6% of the available memory headroom and incurs at most one OOM event while VPA reclaims -7.9% over the same runs, raising memory provisioning, and its recommendation would have been insufficient to avoid OOM in 30 runs. The results demonstrate that an observation-driven RL recommender could outperform retrospective heuristics for dynamic memory scaling.

</details>


### [17] [P-GADMM: Parallel Group-Based ADMM for Asynchronous Optimization in Heterogeneous Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20006)
*Gaiguo Wei,Qingying Zhang,Heqiang Wang,Yu Zhang,Xiaoxiong Zhong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出P-GADMM，一种面向异构边缘网络的并行分组ADMM方法，通过计算感知分组与有界异步云端协调，降低训练时间并保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADMM广泛用于分布式优化，但其同步实现在异构边缘网络中效率低下：快客户端需等待慢客户端才能完成全局更新。现有分组式ADMM虽降低通信开销，但分组规则多基于数据相似性或网络拓扑，未显式考虑计算异构性。

Method: P-GADMM依据客户端计算能力与本地数据规模构建计算感知边缘分组，以减小组内训练速度差异；同时在边缘层做聚合，并在云端引入有界异步协调，让活跃组无需等待慢组即可参与全局更新，并通过延迟阈值控制过时组信息。

Result: 针对强凸组目标，在组级有界过时条件下证明了理想化P-GADMM的收敛性，呈现时间平均收敛行为，收敛至由过时引起的渐近误差邻域；实验表明P-GADMM相较代表性基线减少了墙钟训练时间，同时保持相当的最终精度。

Conclusion: 计算感知分组与有界异步协调可有效缓解异构边缘网络中的等待瓶颈，在保证收敛性与精度的前提下提升分布式ADMM的训练效率。

Abstract: The Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) is widely used for distributed optimization, but its synchronous implementation can suffer from efficiency loss in heterogeneous edge networks, where fast clients or groups need to wait for slower ones before global updates can be completed. Existing group-based ADMM methods reduce communication overhead through grouping, but their grouping rules usually focus on data similarity or network topology and do not explicitly account for computation heterogeneity. To address this issue, this paper proposes Parallel Group-Based ADMM (P-GADMM) for distributed optimization in heterogeneous edge networks. P-GADMM forms computation-aware edge groups according to client computational capabilities and local data sizes, which reduces training-speed variation within each group. It further combines edge-level aggregation with bounded asynchronous coordination at the cloud, allowing active groups to participate in global updates without waiting for slower groups while controlling stale group information through a delay threshold. For strongly convex group objectives, we establish convergence guarantees for an idealized form of P-GADMM under bounded group-level staleness, showing a time-averaged convergence behavior up to a staleness-induced asymptotic error neighborhood. Experiments show that P-GADMM reduces wall-clock training time compared with representative baselines while maintaining comparable final accuracy.

</details>


### [18] [Distributed Edge Inference: an Experimental Study on Multiview Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20009)
*Gianluca Mittone,Giulio Malenza,Marco Aldinucci,Robert Birke*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文围绕"计算连续体"（compute continuum）这一去中心化基础设施范式，通过真实的多视角检测模型 MvDet 与 FastFL 高性能边缘推理框架进行实证验证，在多组边缘算力与网络带宽场景下最多获得 1.92 倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着应用规模增长，云计算所采用的集中式客户端-服务器架构日益限制应用可扩展性。为实现超大规模扩展，云、边缘与雾计算融合为计算连续体，将基础设施彻底去中心化以覆盖泛在资源；但在此复杂环境下设计能获益的应用成为具有挑战性的研究问题，本文旨在检验计算连续体所带来的机会。

Method: 采用真实世界的多视角检测模型（MvDet），并在 FastFL 这一 C/C++ 高性能边缘推理框架上实现，通过设置多种实验场景（涵盖不同边缘计算能力与网络带宽）来评估计算性能。

Result: 在使用相同设备的条件下，相比集中式方案，推理时间最多获得 1.92 倍的加速。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了计算连续体在提升推理性能方面的潜力，证明了去中心化的云/边/雾协同架构对复杂应用具有实际收益。

Abstract: Computing is evolving rapidly to cater to the increasing demand for sophisticated services, and Cloud computing lays a solid foundation for flexible on-demand provisioning. However, as the size of applications grows, the centralised client-server approach used by Cloud computing increasingly limits the applications' scalability. To achieve ultra-scalability, cloud/edge/fog computing converges into the compute continuum, completely decentralising the infrastructure to encompass universal, pervasive resources. The compute continuum makes devising applications benefitting from this complex environment a challenging research problem. We put the opportunities the compute continuum offers to the test through a real-world multi-view detection model (MvDet) implemented with the FastFL C/C++ high-performance edge inference framework. Computational performance is discussed considering many experimental scenarios, encompassing different edge computational capabilities and network bandwidths. We obtain up to 1.92x speedup in inference time over a centralised solution using the same devices.

</details>


### [19] [The Price of Decentralization, Paid Twice: A Latency Floor and a Minimal Subsidy from a Geographic Decentralization Invariant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20322)
*Ruiyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文将“延迟感知的验证者选择导致地理集中化”这一矛盾形式化为协议无关的定理：在 (rho,gamma)-去中心化质押配置下，任意提案者的 beta-仲裁提交延迟至少为 rho（平面上最小值为 rho*sqrt(beta/gamma)），并证明维持去中心化目标需要 Theta(c*rho/sqrt(gamma)) 的反集中补贴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延迟感知的验证者选择机制（通过投票权重、领导权或快速仲裁成员资格奖励低延迟验证者）会促使验证者共址部署，从而侵蚀拜占庭容错所假设的地理故障独立性。论文旨在为这一「延迟奖励 versus 地理去中心化」的张力给出一个尖锐且与具体协议无关的形式化刻画。

Method: 定义 (rho,gamma)-去中心化质押配置：任意半径 rho 区域内持有的质押比例不超过 gamma。在仲裁阈值 beta > gamma 条件下，证明任何提案者的 beta-仲裁提交延迟下界为 rho（与协议无关），并在平面几何中给出紧确最小值 rho*sqrt(beta/gamma)。进一步建立位置选择博弈，采用真实仲裁延迟奖励，将中心化刻画为超临界 O(2) 对称性破缺分岔，并推导维持去中心化目标所需的反集中补贴规模。

Result: 几何下界被证明是初等的；核心贡献是延迟—补贴耦合：去中心化被「支付两次」，一次在延迟、一次在激励，且两者之比为常数。数值验证支持所有命题；在地理实测上，719 个验证者的 Solana 普查配合 RIPE-Atlas 延迟矩阵显示验证者中本聪系数为 19，但地理中本聪系数仅为 1–2。

Conclusion: 论文明确界定其范围：几何下界本身是初等的，真正的贡献在于揭示了延迟与补贴之间的耦合关系，即地理去中心化在延迟与激励两方面以固定比例付出代价，且仅有极低的实际地理去中心化水平。

Abstract: Latency-aware validator selection---rewarding validators for low network latency through voting weight, leadership, or fast-quorum membership---is known to push validators to co-locate, eroding the geographic fault-independence that Byzantine fault tolerance assumes. We give this tension a sharp, protocol-agnostic form. Calling a stake configuration (rho,gamma)-decentralized if no radius-rho region holds more than a gamma fraction of stake, we prove that for quorum threshold beta>gamma every proposer's beta-quorum commit latency is at least rho---independent of the protocol---and in the plane the minimum is exactly rho*sqrt(beta/gamma). Our main result couples this geometric floor to an incentive one: in a location-choice game with the true quorum-latency reward, centralization arises as a supercritical O(2)-symmetry-breaking bifurcation, and sustaining a decentralization target costs an anti-concentration subsidy of magnitude Theta(c*rho/sqrt(gamma))---decentralization is paid twice, in latency and in incentives, in a constant ratio. We verify every claim numerically and on measured geography: a 719-validator Solana census on a RIPE-Atlas latency matrix has validator Nakamoto coefficient 19 but geographic Nakamoto 1--2. We are explicit about scope: the floor is elementary; the contribution is the latency--subsidy coupling.

</details>


### [20] [Accelerating Sharded Data Parallelism at Scale with Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20359)
*Gianluca Mittone,Marco Aldinucci*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该工作借鉴联邦学习的高效通信思想，提出 FL+FSDP 与 FL+HSDP 两种混合算法，将分片数据并行与 FedAvg 式聚合交错结合，把大规模数据并行部署解耦为松耦合的联邦组，从而在降低组间通信的同时限定全局批大小，并在 Llama3.1 8B、512 张 A100 上验证了其可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型训练需在数千张 GPU 上耗时数月，分片数据并行(DP)虽为主流加速手段，但在大规模、尤其是异构多级互连上会产生难以承受的通信开销。作者受联邦学习高效通信原则启发，试图缓解这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出 FL+FSDP 与 FL+HSDP 两种混合算法，将分片 DP 与 FedAvg 式聚合并行交错：把大规模 DP 部署解耦为多个较小的、松耦合的联邦组，仅需极少的组间流量，同时使全局批大小受组规模约束；并对通信代价进行形式化分析。

Result: 在 512 张 A100 上进行 Llama3.1 8B 预训练，在相同超参下，FL+FSDP 与 FL+HSDP 相比对应基线数据处理速度快达 8.04 倍，评估困惑度低至 4.48，显示出更优的计算效率与模型质量。

Conclusion: 性能与质量的优势源于通信开销的降低以及全局批大小相对联邦组规模的受限增长，形式化分析与实验共同证明了方法的可扩展性与灵活性。

Abstract: The symbiotic scaling of artificial intelligence models and high-performance computing systems continually creates algorithmic challenges in their convergence. Foundation models (FMs) are a crucial example, requiring months-long training on thousands of cutting-edge GPUs. Sharded data parallelism (DP) is the dominant strategy to accelerate such computations by splitting data and models across multiple GPUs. However, it incurs prohibitive communication overhead when deployed at scale, particularly on multi-tier interconnects with heterogeneous performance. Inspired by the efficient communication principles of federated learning (FL), this work introduces two hybrid algorithms - FL+FSDP and FL+HSDP - interleaving sharded DP with FedAvg-style aggregations. Such approaches decouple large DP deployments into smaller, loosely-coupled federation groups, requiring minimal inter-group traffic while keeping the global batch size bounded by the groups' size. Formal analysis of communication costs and experimental validation prove their scalability and flexibility. A Llama3.1 8B pre-training on 512 A100 GPUs shows that, under identical hyperparameters, FL+FSDP and FL+HSDP achieve up to 8.04 faster data processing and 4.48 lower evaluation perplexity than their counterparts, demonstrating superior computational efficiency and improved model quality. These properties stem from reduced communication overhead and the bounded growth of the global batch size relative to the federation group size.

</details>


### [21] [A Kubernetes-Native Request Router for Quality-Aware Inference Serving in the Computing Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20497)
*Ignjat Karanovic,Pantelis A. Frangoudis,Ivan Čilić,Ivana Podnar Žarko,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ASRB是一种面向Kubernetes计算连续体、基于分数的自适应请求路由机制，联合考虑基础设施、响应时间和应用级质量指标，可灵活权衡延迟与精度，并降低监控开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算连续体上部署ML等服务时，需要在延迟与精度之间权衡，同时大规模部署的监控开销较大，现有路由机制难以兼顾灵活性与低开销。

Method: ASRB基于加权QoS准则组合的服务商策略进行评分路由，跨多个系统层监控运行时指标，并选择性、自适应地控制监控强度；实现上无需修改Kubernetes。

Result: 在降低延迟配置下，平均响应时间比面向延迟的先进路由机制至少低10 ms；优先精度时可达更高精度；同时失败率更低、对运行环境变化响应更快，监控成本最多降低约70%，部分配置仅有适度延迟代价。

Conclusion: ASRB能够实现灵活、可由运营方控制的延迟-精度权衡，并显著降低监控成本，便于在现有集群中部署和运行。

Abstract: We introduce Adaptive Score-based Routing Balancer (ASRB), a dynamic, score-based request routing mechanism for Kubernetes-based service deployments over the computing continuum. ASRB jointly considers infrastructure-level information, response time measurements, and application-level quality indicators, with a particular focus on serving Machine Learning (ML) workloads. For these workloads, ASRB balances requests over service instances deployed in the continuum, following service provider-defined policies encoded as weighted combinations of QoS criteria to flexibly address latency-accuracy trade-offs. To drive routing decisions and swiftly adapt to changes in the operating environment, ASRB monitors a range of runtime metrics across multiple system layers. To deal with the associated monitoring overhead, particularly important for large-scale deployments, it selectively and adaptively controls monitoring intensity without sacrificing on routing quality. ASRB is implemented without requiring any modifications to Kubernetes, making it straightforward to deploy and operate in existing cluster environments. Our testbed experiments demonstrate the versatility of ASRB: When tuned for latency reduction, it achieves at least 10 ms lower mean response time compared with latency-oriented state-of-the-art routing mechanisms, while it achieves higher accuracy when this is prioritized through specific configurations, thus enabling flexible and operator-controllable trade-offs. At the same time, it attains reduced failure rates, higher responsiveness to changes in the operating environment, and up to ~70% less monitoring cost than relevant state-of-the-art solutions, at the potential expense of only a modest latency penalty in some configurations.

</details>


### [22] [PixelFlow: Token-Level Workload Management for Efficient Distributed DiT Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20723)
*Zhexiang Zhang,Minchen Yu,Yifan Sun,Xu Bai,Xingliang Yuan,Adel N. Toosi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PixelFlow 通过 token 级工作负载管理实现分布式 DiT 在线服务，提升 GPU 利用率与 SLO 达成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线 DiT 图像生成需同时满足延迟 SLO 与 GPU 高效利用。现有系统采用请求级批处理，对 batch size 控制能力有限：batch 过小无法饱和 GPU 计算，过大则违反延迟 SLO；同时全局协调调度要求各 GPU 同步后才能接纳新请求，引入额外延迟。

Method: 提出 PixelFlow，以图像 token 为粒度切分并批处理请求工作负载，使每块 GPU 可在延迟约束下承担部分额外工作，并跨 GPU 分配这些部分以提升并发。系统提供运行时将请求切分为可变大小分区并高效批处理；通过优化 token 放置减少跨 GPU 数据交换并平衡负载；利用去噪步间相似性将剩余传输与计算重叠；采用 SLO 感知调度器按兼容延迟需求将 GPU 分组，各组独立推进，仅在需合并资源接纳新请求时同步。

Result: 在 H100 GPU 上使用 Stable Diffusion 3 与 FLUX.1-dev 评估，PixelFlow 将 SLO 达成率最高提升 43%，goodput 最高达到当前最优 DiT 服务系统的 2.8 倍。

Conclusion: token 级细粒度工作负载管理可有效突破请求级批处理的限制，在满足延迟约束的同时显著提高分布式 DiT 服务的 GPU 利用率与吞吐。

Abstract: Online image generation with Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) must meet latency service-level objectives (SLOs) while using GPU resources efficiently. Existing systems improve GPU utilization by batching multiple requests for joint execution. However, request-level batching offers limited control over batch size: batches may be too small to saturate GPU compute, while larger ones may violate latency SLOs. Globally coordinated scheduling introduces further delays by requiring independently progressing GPUs to synchronize before admitting new work.
  We present PixelFlow, a distributed DiT serving system that addresses these limitations through token-level workload management. Its key idea is to use image tokens (the units a DiT processes to generate an image) to divide and batch request workloads at a finer granularity. This allows each GPU to take on a portion of additional work under latency constraints. By distributing these portions across GPUs, PixelFlow accommodates more concurrent requests, improving GPU utilization while reducing queueing delays. To realize this flexibility, PixelFlow provides a runtime that splits requests into variable-sized partitions and batches them efficiently on each GPU. To reduce the resulting communication overhead, it optimizes token placement to limit cross-GPU data exchange while balancing GPU workloads. It further exploits similarity across denoising steps to overlap the remaining transfers with computation. An SLO-aware scheduler groups GPUs to share compute resources among requests with compatible latency requirements. Each group progresses independently, synchronizing with others only when their combined resources are needed to admit a new request. Evaluation with Stable Diffusion 3 and FLUX.1-dev on H100 GPUs shows that PixelFlow improves SLO attainment by up to 43% and achieves up to 2.8 times the goodput of state-of-the-art DiT serving systems.

</details>
